 | ุਕΥԻʿਠᚑ | Business combinations and goodwill


 | ุਕΥԻમ͜ϗᒅجɝሪfᔷᜫ˾ | Business combinations are accounted for using
 | ᄆܲϗᒅ˚ಂٙʮʪᄆ࠽ࠇඎd༈ | the acquisition method. The consideration
 | ʮʪᄆ࠽މ͉ණྠᔷᜫٙ༟ପe͉ | transferred is measured at the acquisition date fair
 | ණྠΣ஗ϗᒅ˙ۃኹϞɛוዄٙࠋ | value which is the sum of the acquisition date fair
 | වd˸ʿ͉ණྠމ౬՟஗ϗᒅ˙છ | values of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities
 | ՓᛆϾ೯Бٰٙᛆ׵ϗᒅ˚ಂٙʮ | assumed by the Group to the former owners of
 | ʪᄆ࠽ʘᐼձf࿁׵ӊɓධุਕΥ | the acquiree and the equity interests issued by
 | Իd͉ණྠ፯኿˸ʮʪᄆ࠽א஗ϗ | the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree.
 | ᒅ˙̙ᗆйଋ༟ପٙˢԷ΅ᕘࠇඎ | For each business combination, the Group elects
 | ஗ϗᒅ˙ٙڢછٰᛆूfڢછٰᛆ | whether to measure the non-controlling interests
 | ूٙהϞՉ˼ଡ଼ϓ௅ʱѩܲʮʪᄆ | in the acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate
 | ࠽ࠇඎfϗᒅ޴ᗫϓ͉׵೯͛ࣛࠇ | share of the acquiree ’s identifiable net assets. All
 | ɝ຅ಂฦूf | other components of non-controlling interests are
 | measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs
 | are expensed as incurred.


 | ຅ϗᒅٙɓଡ଼ݺਗʿ༟ପ̍ўɓධ | The Group determines that it has acquired a
 | ҳɝʿɓධྼሯ׌ཀ೻d˲Շ٫΍ | business when the acquired set of activities and
 | Ν࿁௴ிପ̈ٙঐɢϞࠠɽ্ᘠ | assets includes an input and a substantive process
 | ࣛd͉ණྠᆽ֛Չϗᒅəɓධุਕf | that together significantly contribute to the ability
 | to create outputs.


 | ຅͉ණྠϗᒅɓධุਕࣛdՉึ࣬ | When the Group acquires a business, it assesses
 | ኽϗᒅ˚ಂٙΥߒૢಛe຾᏶ᐑྤ | the financial assets and liabilities assumed for
 | ʿ޴ᗫૢ΁d൙Пהוટٙږፄ༟ | appropriate classification and designation in
 | ପʿࠋව˸ආБቇ຅ٙʱᗳʿܸ֛f | accordance with the contractual terms, economic
 | circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the
 | acquisition date.


 | ϗᒅ˙ᔷᜫٙ΂Оא್˾ᄆ׵ϗᒅ | Any contingent consideration to be transferred
 | ˚ಂܲʮʪᄆ࠽ᆽႩfʱᗳމ༟ପ | by the acquirer is recognised at fair value at
 | אࠋවٙא್˾ᄆܲʮʪᄆ࠽ࠇ | the acquisition date. Contingent consideration
 | ඎdʮʪᄆ࠽ᜊਗࠇɝฦूfʱᗳ | classified as an asset or liability is measured at fair
 | މᛆूٙא್˾ᄆʔʚࠠอࠇඎd | value with changes in fair value recognised in profit
 | Չܝᚃഐၑ׵ᛆूʫɝሪf | or loss. Contingent consideration that is classified
 | as equity is not remeasured and subsequent
 | settlement is accounted for within equity.

 | ਠᚑڋ֐ܲϓ͉ࠇඎdϓ͉މᔷᜫ | Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the

 | ආБ಻༊f͉ණྠ׵ӊϋ | 12  ˜ | 31indicate that the carrying value may be impaired.
 | ˚࿁ਠᚑආБϋܓಯ࠽಻༊fఱಯ | The Group performs its annual impairment test
 | ࠽಻༊ϾԊd׵ุਕΥԻʕϗᒅٙ | of goodwill as at 31 December. For the purpose
 | ਠᚑІϗᒅ˚ಂৎ஗ʱৣЇཫಂਗ਼ | of impairment testing, goodwill acquired in a

 | ΢ତږପ̈ఊʩאତږପ̈ఊʩଡ଼ | allocated to each of the Group  ’s cash-generating

 | or liabilities of the Group are assigned to those

 | ಯ࠽ஷཀ൙Пਠᚑ޴ᗫٙତږପ̈ | Impairment is determined by assessing the

 | ගᔷΫf | amount, an impairment loss is recognised. An

 | ʮʪᄆ࠽ࠇඎ | Fair value measurement


 | ͉ණྠ׵΢జѓಂ͋ܲʮʪᄆ࠽ࠇ | The Group measures its certain financial assets
 | ඎՉ߰ʍږፄ༟ପʿࠋවfʮʪᄆ | and liabilities at fair value at the end of each
 | ࠽މ׵ࠇඎ˚ಂί̹ఙਞၾ٫ගආ | reporting period. Fair value is the price that would
 | БٙϞҏʹ׸ʕ̈ਯ༟ପהϗ՟א | be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a
 | ᔷ୅ࠋවה˕˹ٙᄆࣸfʮʪᄆ࠽ | liability in an orderly transaction between market
 | ࠇඎɗ৿ண̈ਯ༟ପאᔷ୅ࠋවٙ | participants at the measurement date. The fair
 | ʹ׸׵༟ପאࠋවٙ˴ࠅ̹ఙආ | value measurement is based on the presumption
 | Бdאί͊Ϟ˴ࠅ̹ఙٙઋرɨd | that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer
 | ۆ৿ண׵༟ପאࠋවٙ௰Ϟл̹ఙ | the liability takes place either in the principal
 | ආБf˴ࠅא௰Ϟл̹ఙ̀඲މ͉ | market for the asset or liability, or in the absence
 | ණྠ̙ආɝ̹ٙఙf༟ପאࠋවٙ | of a principal market, in the most advantageous
 | ʮʪᄆ࠽ɗਿ׵̹ఙਞၾ٫މ༟ପ | market for the asset or liability. The principal or the
 | אࠋව֛ᄆࣛהٙ͜৿ணࠇඎ৿ண | most advantageous market must be accessible by
 | ̹ఙਞၾ٫˸ୌΥ־ഃ௰Գ຾᏶л | the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability
 | ूٙ˙όБԫf | is measured using the assumptions that market
 | participants would use when pricing the asset or
 | liability, assuming that market participants act in
 | their economic best interest.

 | ޴࿁ዹͭਯᄆਿ๟ਗ਼Υߒʕٙ˾ᄆ | non-lease components, the Group allocates the
 | ʱৣЇ΢ଡ଼ϓ௅ʱfॡږϗɝܲॡ | consideration in the contract to each component
 | ༣ಂʫٜٙᇞجᆽႩdԨΪՉ຾ᐄ | on a relative stand-alone selling price basis. Rental
 | ׌ሯϾࠇɝฦूڌʕٙՉ˼ϗɝʿ | income is accounted for on a straight-line basis
 | ϗूfί՘ਠձτર຾ᐄॡ༣ࣛ೯ | over the lease term and is included in other income
 | ͛ٙڋ֐ٜટϓ͉dࠇɝॡ༣༟ପ | and gains in the statement of profit or loss due to
 | ٙሪࠦ࠽dԨίॡ༣ಂʫܲၾॡږ | its operating nature. Initial direct costs incurred
 | ϗɝ޴ΝٙਿᓾᆽႩfא್ॡږί | in negotiating and arranging an operating lease
 | ᒃ՟ಂගᆽႩމϗɝf | are added to the carrying amount of the leased

2. | ึࠇ݁ഄᚃ | 2. | ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)

 | 2.4 | ࠠɽึࠇ݁ഄᚃ | 2.4 | MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES
 | (Continued)

 | ږፄ༟ପಯ࠽ᚃ | Impairment of financial assets (Continued)

 | ɓছ˙ج | General approach

 | ཫಂڦ͜ฦ̰׵ՇࡈචݬආБᆽ | ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit
 | Ⴉf࿁׵ІڋӉᆽႩܝԨೌᜑഹᄣ | exposures for which there has not been a
 | ̋ٙڦ൲ࠬᎈdཫಂڦ͜ฦ̰ఱ̙ | significant increase in credit risk since initial
 | ঐ׵͊Ը | 12 ࡈ˜ʫ12 | ࡈ˜ཫಂڦ | recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses
 | ͜ฦ̰̈ତٙ༼ߒԫ΁הኬߧٙڦ | that result from default events that are possible
 | within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For
 | ͜ฦ̰ࠇ౤ᅡ௪f࿁׵ІڋӉᆽႩ | those credit exposures for which there has been
 | ܝϞᜑഹᄣ̋ٙڦ൲ࠬᎈd඲ίڦ | a significant increase in credit risk since initial
 | ͜ฦ̰ࠬᎈཫಂٙ௵ቱϋಂࠇ౤ᑦ | recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit
 | ฦᅡ௪dʔሞ༼ߒԫ΁׵Оࣛ೯͛ | losses expected over the remaining life of the
 | Όಂཫಂڦ͜ฦ̰f | exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
 | lifetime ECL).

 | ׵΢జѓ˚ಂd͉ණྠ׵൙ПІڋ | At each reporting date, the Group assesses
 | ӉᆽႩܝږፄʈՈٙڦ൲ࠬᎈ݊щ | whether the credit risk on a financial instrument
 | has increased significantly since initial recognition.
 | ᜑഹᄣ̋ࣛdਗ਼׵జѓ˚ಂږፄʈ | When making the assessment, the Group
 | Ո೯͛༼ߒٙࠬᎈၾڋӉᆽႩ˚ಂ | compares the risk of a default occurring on the
 | ږፄʈՈ೯͛༼ߒٙࠬᎈආБˢ | financial instrument as at the reporting date with
 | ༰dԨϽᅇೌც˹̈ʔ̀ࠅٙϓ͉ | the risk of a default occurring on the financial
 | אрɢу̙੻ٙΥଣ˲̙˕ܵٙ༟ | instrument as at the date of initial recognition
 | ࣘdܼ̍ዝ̦ʿۃᓼ׌༟ࣘf͉ණ | and considers reasonable and supportable
 | ྠႩމd຅Υߒ˹ಛགྷಂ൴ཀ30˂ | information that is available without undue cost
 | ࣛdڦ൲ࠬᎈᜑഹᄣ̋f | or effort, including historical and forward-looking
 | information. The Group considers that there has
 | been a significant increase in credit risk when
 | contractual payments are more than 30 days past
 | due.

 | ͉ණྠਗ਼Υߒ˹ಛགྷಂ 90˚ٙږፄ | The Group considers a financial asset in default
 | ༟ପൖЪ༼ߒf್Ͼd׵߰ʍઋر | when contractual payments are 90 days past due.
 | ɨd຅ʫ௅א̮௅༟ࣘᜑ͉ͪණྠ | However, in certain cases, the Group may also
 | ʔ˄̙ঐί͉ණྠમ՟΂Оڦ൲౤ | consider a financial asset to be in default when
 | ʺτરۃ઄ᅰϗΫ͊ᎵᒔΥߒږᕘ | internal or external information indicates that

 | ࣛd͉ණྠ͵̙ঐႩމ༈ږፄ༟ପ | contractual amounts in full before taking into
 | ༼ߒf | account any credit enhancements held by the

 | אڢᓥ᙮ૢ΁dۆ̥ࠅהϞՉ˼ุ | Where awards include a market or non-vesting

 | ҳ༟يุၾІ͜גήପٙʱᗳ | Classification between investment properties and

 | ͉ණྠᆽ֛ɓධيุ݊щୌΥҳ༟يุٙ | The Group determines whether a property qualifies as
 | ૢ΁dԨʊՓ֛Ъ̈༈кᓙٙᅺ๟fҳ༟ | an investment property, and has developed criteria in
 | يุܸ݊މᒃ՟ॡږאމ༟͉ᄣ࠽אՇ٫ | making that judgement. An investment property is a
 | ࡒϞϾܵϞٙيุfΪϤd͉ණྠึϽᅇ | property held to earn rentals or for capital appreciation
 | ɓධيุ݊щίܘɽ೻ܓɪዹͭ׵͉ණྠ | or both. Therefore, the Group considers whether a
 | ܵϞٙՉ˼༟ପϾପ͛ତږݴඎfϞԬي | property generates cash flows largely independently of
 | ุٙɓ௅ʱ݊މᒃ՟ॡږא༟͉ᄣ࠽Ͼܵ | the other assets held by the Group. Some properties
 | Ϟd̤ɓ௅ʱ݊މ͜׵͛ପא౤Զਠۜא | comprise a portion that is held to earn rentals or for
 | ؂ਕאމБ݁၍ଣͦٙϾܵϞfν؈வԬ | capital appreciation and another portion that is held for
 | ௅ʱঐ੄ఊዹ̈ਯאஷཀፄ༟ॡ༣ఊዹ̈ | use in the production or supply of goods or services or
 | ॡd͉ණྠਗ਼ʱй࿁வԬ௅ʱආБึࠇஈ | for administrative purposes. If these portions could be
 | ଣfν؈வԬ௅ʱʔঐఊዹ̈ਯdۆසί | sold separately or leased out separately under a finance
 | މ͜׵͛ପא౤Զਠۜא؂ਕאމБ݁၍ | lease, the Group accounts for the portions separately. If
 | ଣͦٙϾܵϞٙ௅ʱʔࠠɽٙઋرɨd༈ | the portions could not be sold separately, the property
 | يุʑ஗ᓥᗳމҳ༟يุf͉ණྠึ࣬ኽ | is an investment property only if an insignificant portion

 | ࠠɽՑԴ༈يุʔୌΥҳ༟يุٙૢ΁f | services or for administrative purposes. Judgement
 | is made on an individual property basis to determine
 | whether ancillary services are so significant that a
 | property does not qualify as an investment property.

 | ڢږፄ༟ପਠᚑৰ̮ٙಯ࠽ | Impairment of non-financial assets (other than goodwill)

 | ͉ණྠ׵΢జѓಂ͋൙ПՉהϞڢږፄ༟ | The Group assesses whether there are any indicators
 | ପܼ̍Դ͜ᛆ༟ପ݊щϞ΂Оಯ࠽༦ | of impairment for all non-financial assets (including

 | אତږପ͛ఊЗٙሪࠦ࠽൴̈Չ̙ϗΫږ | when there are indicators that the carrying amounts
 | ᕘуՉʮʪᄆ࠽ಯϓ͉ၾԴ͜ᄆ࠽Շ٫ | may not be recoverable. An impairment exists when
 | ٙ༰৷٫ࣛdӱତಯ࠽fʮʪᄆ࠽ಯ | the carrying value of an asset or a cash-generating unit
 | ϓ͉ɗ࣬ኽ੽ᗳЧ༟ପʮ̻ʹ׸ٙՈߒҼ | exceeds its recoverable amount, which is the higher
 | ɢٙቖਯʹ׸ה՟੻ᅰኽא̙ᝈ࿀̹ఙᄆ | of its fair value less costs of disposal and its value
 | ࣸಯ̘̈ਯ༟ପٙ჈ᄣϓ͉Ͼࠇၑf຅ࠇ | in use. The calculation of the fair value less costs of
 | ၑԴ͜ᄆ࠽ࣛd၍ଣᄴ඲Пࠇ̙੽༈༟ପ | disposal is based on available data from binding sales
 | אତږପ͛ఊЗ՟੻ٙཫಂ͊Ըତږݴ | transactions in an arm | ’s length transaction of similar
 | ඎdԨ፯͜Υቇٙ൨ତଟd˸ࠇၑ༈ഃତ | assets or observable market prices less incremental
 | ږݴඎٙତ࠽fيุeᅀגʿண௪eҳ༟ | costs for disposing of the asset. When value in use
 | يุeԴ͜ᛆ༟ପeՉ˼ೌҖ༟ପe׵ᑌ | calculations are undertaken, management must
 | ᐄʮ̡ٙҳ༟ʿՉ˼ڢݴਗ༟ପٙሪࠦ࠽ | estimate the expected future cash flows from the asset
 | ༱׵ৌਕజڌڝൗ | 14e | 15e | 16e | 18e | 19or cash-generating unit and choose a suitable discount
 | ʿ 21f | rate in order to calculate the present value of those

 | equipment, investment properties, right-of-use assets,
 | other intangible assets, investment in associates and
 | other non-current assets are set out in notes 14, 15, 16,
 | 18, 19 and 21 to the financial statements.

 | ਠᚑಯ࠽ | Impairment of goodwill


 | ͉ණྠЇˇӊϋᆽ֛ਠᚑ݊щ೯͛ಯ࠽f | The Group determines whether goodwill is impaired at
 | வცࠅ࿁ਠᚑהʱৣٙତږପ̈ఊʩٙԴ | least on an annual basis. This requires an estimation of
 | ͜ᄆ࠽ආБПࠇfПࠇԴ͜ᄆ࠽ࠅӋ͉ණ | the value in use of the cash-generating units to which
 | ྠ࿁ԸІତږପ̈ఊʩٙཫಂ͊Ըତږݴ | the goodwill is allocated. Estimating the value in use
 | ඎЪ̈ПࠇdԨ፯኿Υቇٙұତଟ˸ࠇၑ | requires the Group to make an estimate of the expected
 | ༈ഃତږݴඎٙତ࠽f׵ | 2025 | ϋ  12 ˜  31 | future cash flows from the cash-generating units and
 | ˚dਠᚑٙሪࠦ࠽މɛ͏࿆ | 91,850,000 | ʩ | also to choose a suitable discount rate in order to
 | 2024 | ϋjೌfආɓӉ༉ઋ༱׵ڝൗ17f | calculate the present value of those cash flows. The
